([specscore/ai-plugin-specscore](https://github.com/specscore/ai-plugin-specscore/tree/HEAD/skills/task))
- **specset**: Core CLI for Specset — install or update the CLI, authenticate with a browser or device code, switch organizations, and run GraphQL with `specset api`. Start here for CLI setup or raw GraphQL access, and whenever a specset command fails with auth or org errors. ([specsetai/specset-plugins](https://github.com/specsetai/specset-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/specset/skills/specset))
- **specset-admin**: Use when administering a Specset organization — inviting and managing members and roles, or applying whitelabel branding (colors, logos) — for org membership, permissions, or appearance tasks. ([specsetai/specset-plugins](https://github.com/specsetai/specset-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/specset/skills/specset-admin))
- **specset-agent**: Ask Specset's in-project AI agent questions that need deep project understanding — schedules, spec interpretation, cross-document analysis — via `specset agent chat`. Use when raw GraphQL isn't enough or when unsure how to answer from data. ([specsetai/specset-plugins](https://github.com/specsetai/specset-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/specset/skills/specset-agent))
- **specset-closeout**: Use when managing Specset closeout and facilities data — assets, locations, products, companies, warranties, procedures, maintenance plans, work orders, and their documents and links. ([specsetai/specset-plugins](https://github.com/specsetai/specset-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/specset/skills/specset-closeout))
- **specset-issues**: Use when working with a Specset project's design-review issues from the CLI — listing the queue by severity, tier, or verification state, and reading each issue's verdict. ([specsetai/specset-plugins](https://github.com/specsetai/specset-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/specset/skills/specset-issues))
- **specset-projects**: Use when standing up a new Specset project or adding document sets to one — create the project, upload spec/drawing PDFs, monitor processing, and publish spec sets and drawing sets. ([specsetai/specset-plugins](https://github.com/specsetai/specset-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/specset/skills/specset-projects))
- **specset-rfis**: Manage Specset RFIs — list, search, create, update, and record responses including the official answer of record. Use for any RFI logging or tracking task. ([specsetai/specset-plugins](https://github.com/specsetai/specset-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/specset/skills/specset-rfis))
- **specset-search**: Use when the user asks to find, look up, or locate anything in their Specset projects — semantic and keyword search across specs, drawing sheets, submittals, RFIs, documents, and closeout records. ([specsetai/specset-plugins](https://github.com/specsetai/specset-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/specset/skills/specset-search))
- **specset-submittals**: Manage Specset submittals — list, search, create, revise, attach files, track approvers, and run AI spec-compliance reviews. Use for any submittal log or submittal review task. ([specsetai/specset-plugins](https://github.com/specsetai/specset-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/specset/skills/specset-submittals))
- **specset-tools**: Use when an agent should call Specset's own retrieval tools directly — semantic/keyword search and structured reads over specs, drawings, submittals, RFIs, documents, closeout, and schedule — via `specset tools` or a `specset mcp` server. Deeper than raw GraphQL search; lighter than delegating to the in-app agent. ([specsetai/specset-plugins](https://github.com/specsetai/specset-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/specset/skills/specset-tools))

- **specsfy-04-validate**: Audita se a spec.md da fatia segue o formato Specsfy/2.0 e está pronta para planejar, registra o Definition Gate na seção 13 e conduz o aceite final em review. Use quando o usuário pedir para validar, revisar, auditar ou checar uma spec, quando uma transição automática pedir prova do Definition Gate, nova validação após correção ou aceite da entrega. Não decide requisitos nem cria checklist, relatório ou plan.md: lacunas de decisão vão para specsfy-02-backlog, correções de spec aprovada para specsfy-update-spec e o planejamento para specsfy-05-tasks. ([promovaweb/specsfy](https://github.com/promovaweb/specsfy/tree/HEAD/skills/specsfy-04-validate) · ★90)
- **specsfy-04-validate**: Use quando o usuário pede para validar, revisar, auditar ou checar se `specs/{id}-{slug}/spec.md` segue o formato rígido Specsfy/2.0 e está pronto para planejar. Use também quando uma transição automática pedir prova do Definition Gate ou nova validação após correção. Use antes do Ato II. Registre gates na seção 13 do mesmo arquivo; não crie checklist, relatório, plan.md ou outro artefato. ([promovaweb/specsfy](https://github.com/promovaweb/specsfy/tree/HEAD/example/.agents/skills/specsfy-04-validate) · ★90)
- **specsfy-05-tasks**: Use quando o usuário quer quebrar ou decompor a especificação em tarefas, preencher ou atualizar a seção `14. Tarefas` de `spec.md`, ordenar dependências, planejar fatias verticais ou preparar a execução. Use também quando uma transição automática pedir planejamento, replanejamento ou retomada após RED. Use somente para editar o backlog dentro da fonte única; não crie tasks.md, não escreva código nem marque trabalho como concluído. ([promovaweb/specsfy](https://github.com/promovaweb/specsfy/tree/HEAD/example/.agents/skills/specsfy-05-tasks) · ★90)
- **specsfy-05-tasks**: Decompõe uma spec aprovada em tarefas pequenas, ordenadas e rastreáveis nas seções 14 e 15 da própria spec.md e conduz o Plan Gate. Use quando o usuário quiser quebrar a especificação em tarefas, preencher ou atualizar a seção 14. Tarefas, ordenar dependências, planejar fatias verticais ou preparar a execução, e quando uma transição automática pedir planejamento, replanejamento ou retomada após RED. Edita somente o plano dentro da fonte única: não cria tasks.md, não escreve código e não marca trabalho como concluído. Testes e RED ficam com specsfy-06-tdd-bdd e a execução com specsfy-07-implem… ([promovaweb/specsfy](https://github.com/promovaweb/specsfy/tree/HEAD/skills/specsfy-05-tasks) · ★90)
- **specsfy-06-tdd-bdd**: Use quando o usuário pede TDD, BDD, testes derivados do Gherkin de referência da especificação, rastreabilidade entre `spec.md` e testes, ou um ciclo RED-GREEN-REFACTOR. Use também quando uma transição automática pedir materialização, RED ou verificação de testes. Use para editar as seções 11–13 e executar testes; `specsfy-07-implement` continua responsável pelas tarefas de produção na seção 14 do mesmo arquivo. ([promovaweb/specsfy](https://github.com/promovaweb/specsfy/tree/HEAD/example/.agents/skills/specsfy-06-tdd-bdd) · ★90)
- **specsfy-06-tdd-bdd**: Usa o Gherkin de referência da spec para escrever testes TDD executáveis, provar RED antes da implementação, conduzir RED-GREEN-REFACTOR e auditar a rastreabilidade entre spec.md e testes nas seções 11 a 13. Use quando o usuário pedir TDD, BDD, testes derivados da especificação, rastreabilidade ou um ciclo RED-GREEN-REFACTOR, e quando uma transição automática pedir materialização, RED ou verificação de testes. Não executa Gherkin nem arquivos .feature e não inventa comportamento. O plano de tarefas fica com specsfy-05-tasks e as tarefas de produção da seção 14 com specsfy-07-implement. ([promovaweb/specsfy](https://github.com/promovaweb/specsfy/tree/HEAD/skills/specsfy-06-tdd-bdd) · ★90)
- **specsfy-07-implement**: Use quando o usuário pede para implementar, executar ou concluir tarefas da seção `14. Tarefas` de `specs/{id}-{slug}/spec.md`, criar o código definido pela fonte única, continuar a próxima tarefa pronta ou finalizar a feature. Use também quando uma transição automática iniciar ou retomar a entrega. Use para produção; para apenas criar testes use specsfy-06-tdd-bdd, e para apenas decompor use specsfy-05-tasks. ([promovaweb/specsfy](https://github.com/promovaweb/specsfy/tree/HEAD/example/.agents/skills/specsfy-07-implement) · ★90)
- **specsfy-07-implement**: Executa as tarefas da seção `14. Tarefas` de `spec.md` da feature em ordem de dependência, escreve o código de produção a partir de testes TDD em RED, registra a comprovação na própria spec e fecha o Delivery Gate. Use quando o usuário pedir para implementar, executar, continuar a próxima tarefa pronta ou concluir uma feature já planejada, ou quando uma transição automática iniciar ou retomar a entrega. Não use para apenas criar testes (use specsfy-06-tdd-bdd), apenas decompor tarefas (use specsfy-05-tasks) ou mudar o comportamento acordado (use specsfy-update-spec). ([promovaweb/specsfy](https://github.com/promovaweb/specsfy/tree/HEAD/skills/specsfy-07-implement) · ★90)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-annotation-ranking-evaluation))
- **spectral-annotation-recall-precision-quantification**: Use when you have run two or more annotation pipelines on the same MS/MS spectral dataset (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-annotation-recall-precision-quantification))
- **spectral-array-extraction-and-reconstruction**: Use when your input is an mzPeak archive (ZIP of Parquet files) and you need to recover spectrum signal data (m/z values and intensities) for downstream analysis, visualization, or format conversion. Use this skill when working with profile or centroid mode spectra stored in `spectra_data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-array-extraction-and-reconstruction))
- **spectral-artifact-identification-in-tandem-ms**: Use when you have a raw or preprocessed peak table from tandem MS/MS data (e.g., from Progenesis QI, MS-DIAL, or Bruker Metaboscape) and observe features that may represent detector artifacts, incorrectly merged/split isotopic patterns, or sample carryover contaminants (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-artifact-identification-in-tandem-ms))
- **spectral-assignment-tuning**: Use when a 2D 1H,13C HSQC mixture-spectrum reconstruction pipeline shows parameter sensitivity: you must choose assignment_radius and detection_threshold values, compare single-pass vs incremental MCF variants, or validate that F1 scores remain stable over a plausible parameter grid. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-assignment-tuning))
- **spectral-attribute-support-assessment**: Use when you must decide which R package to adopt for reading or writing mass-spectral libraries in NIST msp / MoNA msp / GNPS mgf dialects, or when compiling a package capability matrix for spectral-file format support. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-attribute-support-assessment))
- **spectral-baseline-correction**: Use when you have loaded raw MSI spectral data (imzML format) in profile or centroid mode and need to remove background noise and baseline artifacts before intensity normalization or ROI analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-baseline-correction))
- **spectral-basis-function-grid-alignment**: Use when two MRS basis sets (e.g., an MRSCloud-simulated set and a reference set such as MARSS or LCModel) must be compared metabolite-by-metabolite and their basis functions first need to be placed on a shared spectral grid. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-basis-function-grid-alignment))
- **spectral-basis-set-comparison**: Use when you have a simulated MRS basis set and a phantom-acquired or vendor-provided basis set for the same sequence/field (e.g., 3T PRESS TE 30 ms) and need per-metabolite agreement metrics such as ICCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-basis-set-comparison))
- **spectral-batch-submission-to-networking-server**: Use when you have deconvolved GC-MS spectra in GNPS_GC input-compatible format and want to construct a molecular network to identify relationships between unknown compounds and perform structured chemical similarity analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-batch-submission-to-networking-server) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-benchmark-table-compilation**: Use when reproducing or extending the MassSpecGym molecule-retrieval benchmark: you have pretrained spectra-to-candidate models, MassSpecGym spectra and candidate datasets, and need per-view rank@1 values aggregated into a comparison table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-benchmark-table-compilation))
- **spectral-bias-orthogonal-polynomial-dynamics**: Use for NN spectral bias via orthogonal polynomial dynamics. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/spectral-bias-orthogonal-polynomial-dynamics) · ★4)
- **spectral-bin-assignment-mapping**: Use when you have calibrated 1D 1H NMR serum/plasma spectra from a Bruker IVDr 600 MHz spectrometer and need to map 24 predefined spin-system bins to metabolite signals in a 22-metabolite panel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-bin-assignment-mapping))
- **spectral-binning-and-totalarea-normalization**: Use when aligned, window-selected spectra (spectra_df rows indexed by ppm) must be converted to a fixed feature table for statistical analysis or classification, e.g. after Icoshift alignment in a Protomix 1H-NMR metabolomics pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-binning-and-totalarea-normalization))
- **spectral-binning-uniform-bucketing**: Use when per-sample NMR spectral data (e.g. 1r processed spectra from fid/acqu(ac)us acquisitions) must be reduced from full spectral resolution to a fixed-width bucketed feature table (exactly 1000 data columns) for downstream analysis tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-binning-uniform-bucketing))
- **spectral-born-machines**: Quantum generative models using group Fourier analysis for discrete data with classical trainability at scale (arXiv: 2607.06675) ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/quantum/spectral-born-machines) · ★4)
- **spectral-bucket-assignment**: Use when you have a collection of mass spectrometry spectral data (m/z and intensity pairs) that must be indexed for fast neighbor retrieval or similarity search, and you need to trade some precision in similarity matching for substantial gains in query speed and memory efficiency across large. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-bucket-assignment))
- **spectral-bucket-intensity-reshaping**: Use when a processed 1D NMR bucket table from NMRProcFlow must be handed off to an online statistical tool (MetaboAnalyst 3.0 / BioStatFlow) and you need a ready-to-import samples-by-buckets matrix with experimental-factor class labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-bucket-intensity-reshaping))
- **spectral-bucket-table-processing**: Use when you have a matrix of processed 1D 1H NMR spectra (ppm scale x samples) from NMRProcFlow/NMRspec and need to reduce them to a bucket table with adaptive intervals and SNR-based quality filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-bucket-table-processing))
- **spectral-bucket-variable-labelling**: Use when a binned NMR bucket intensity matrix (rows of bucket intensities over Bin.SpectraPoint spectral points per sample) plus sample class metadata must be converted into MetaboAnalyst or Workflow4Metabolomics input files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-bucket-variable-labelling))
- **spectral-candidate-classification**: Use when after performing spectral library matching of mass spectrometry peaks against a fragmentation library (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-candidate-classification))
- **spectral-classifier-ensemble-training**: Use when a curated set of annotated and labeled HR-MS/MS spectra of compounds of interest ('relevant') and others ('other') is available and you need a trained ensemble that classifies new MGF spectra into 'relevant' vs 'other' for natural-product prioritization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-classifier-ensemble-training))
- **spectral-cluster-annotation-mapping**: Use when upstream msFeaST result tables (embeddings, k-medoids cluster labels, globaltest statistics, contrast mappings) must be joined per feature and serialized into the schema expected by the msFeaST_Dashboard_bundle.html interactive dashboard. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-cluster-annotation-mapping))
- **spectral-cluster-connectivity-assessment**: Use when after running spectral networking on tandem MS data and obtaining a network graph, when you need to assess which spectra cluster together, determine cluster representatives, and propagate RiPP identifications across clusters at distance 1 or 2 to enlarge the set of identified RiPPs beyond. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/spectral-cluster-connectivity-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-cluster-marking-and-filtering**: Use when reconstructing or validating peak-detection code for simulated 1D 1H, 2D J-Res or COSY metabolomic NMR spectra where peaks must be localized as clusters above a user-defined intensity threshold before downstream spectral simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-cluster-marking-and-filtering))
- **spectral-cluster-medoid-selection**: Use when you have GLEAMS cluster assignments (GLEAMS_cluster.npy) and medoid indexes (GLEAMS_cluster_medoids.npy) and need to pick one representative spectrum per cluster of previously unidentified spectra for downstream search/annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-cluster-medoid-selection))
- **spectral-clustering-and-feature-grouping**: Use when you have raw MS/MS feature data with m/z, retention time, and fragmentation spectra from an untargeted metabolomics experiment, and you need to annotate reaction-derived metabolites of xenobiotics without relying on a priori targeted methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-clustering-and-feature-grouping) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-clustering-assignment**: Use when a fused sample-by-sample similarity network (e.g., from SNF over methylation and metabolomics distance matrices) must be partitioned into subgroups, with cluster labels retained per sample and cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-clustering-assignment))
- **spectral-clustering-cell-grouping**: Use when running Bfimpute on an unlabeled scRNA-seq count matrix and the number of cell types is known (e.g., k=7 for the Chu et al. 2016 dataset), so cells must be grouped before group-wise imputation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-clustering-cell-grouping))
- **spectral-clustering-completeness-quantification**: Use when benchmarking MS/MS clustering tools (msCluster, Falcon, MaRaCluster) on mzML/MGF-derived spectra and you need to report how many spectra end up in usable clusters (N10) and the overall clustered fraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-clustering-completeness-quantification))
- **spectral-clustering-consensus-assignment**: Use when multiple scRNAseq clustering assignments (e.g., Seurat-Louvain, Seurat-SLC, CHOIR, RACEID, SC3, Spectrum, scSHC) for the same cells must be fused into one consensus clustering, and the true number of clusters is unknown. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spectral-clustering-consensus-assignment))
- **spectral-clustering-consensus-spectrum-generation**: Use when you have per-sample MGF MS2 files from untargeted LC-MS/MS metabolomics (e.g., MSV000080502 Euphorbia dendroides data) and need to collapse redundant MS2 scans into clean consensus spectra for GNPS/Tremolo library identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-clustering-consensus-spectrum-generation))
- **spectral-clustering-density-based**: Use when you have computed a sparse pairwise distance matrix from MS/MS spectra (via nearest neighbor indexing) and need to partition spectra into homogeneous clusters—typically when clustering bottom-up proteomics data with the goal of grouping spectra from the same peptide sequence or when you. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/spectral-clustering-density-based) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-clustering-execution**: Use when an scRNA-seq expression matrix needs cell-type clustering via spectral clustering on a learned or enhanced similarity matrix, and clustering quality must be scored with NMI and ARI against provided cell-type labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-clustering-execution))
- **spectral-clustering-laplacian-eigendecomposition**: Use when clustering gene-expression or multi-omic variables into co-expression modules without scale-free constraints, especially when regression slopes and correlation jointly matter and minimal parameter tuning is desired. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spectral-clustering-laplacian-eigendecomposition))
- **spectral-clustering-membership-resolution**: Use when you have predicted BGC-spectrum link scores (e.g., IOKR or correlation values) for individual pairs and need to rank GCF-MF associations where each GCF contains multiple BGCs and each MF contains multiple spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-clustering-membership-resolution))
- **spectral-clustering-module-detection**: Use when you need to partition gene or genus expression data into co-expression modules without assuming scale-free topology, especially when WGCNA yields too few modules or requires heavy parameter tuning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spectral-clustering-module-detection))
- **spectral-clustering-network-analysis**: Use when clustering genes (or other multi-omic variables) into co-expression modules from a normalized expression matrix and the scale-free topology assumption of WGCNA is inappropriate or manual parameter tuning should be avoided. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spectral-clustering-network-analysis))
- **spectral-clustering-of-2d-embeddings**: Use when you have 2D t-SNE or UMAP cell embeddings (original and hyperparameter-optimized) for scRNA-seq data with annotated cell type labels and need an objective cluster-vs-label agreement metric such as ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-clustering-of-2d-embeddings))
- **spectral-clustering-of-as-matrices**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq junction/AS matrices are QC-filtered and imputed and the goal is unsupervised discovery of splicing-defined cell subpopulations that gene-expression clustering misses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-clustering-of-as-matrices))
- **spectral-clustering-of-generated-expression-profiles**: Use when you have GAN-generated single-cell expression profiles produced by mapping spatial transcriptomics data (x1) into the scRNA-seq domain, and you need to verify or derive spatially informed structure at single-cell resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-clustering-of-generated-expression-profiles))
- **spectral-clustering-of-single-cells**: Use when an scRNA-seq count matrix must be partitioned into known or estimated cell-type groups before Bfimpute imputation, and the number of cell types is known (e.g., 7 for Chu et al. 2016). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-clustering-of-single-cells))
- **spectral-clustering-of-splicing-landscapes**: Use when the goal is to define unsupervised cell subpopulations from single-cell RNA-seq junction/AS data (e.g., SMART-seq BAMs), especially where gene-expression clustering fails to separate transitional states such as precancerous epithelium. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-clustering-of-splicing-landscapes))
- **spectral-clustering-on-learned-similarity**: Use when you have a gene-by-cell scRNA-seq expression matrix with known number of cell types and need cell-type clusters whose quality depends on an engineered/learned similarity matrix rather than raw expression distances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-clustering-on-learned-similarity))
- **spectral-clustering-on-similarity-matrix**: Use when you have per-algorithm clustering assignments for scRNAseq cells (e.g. from Seurat-Louvain, SC3, Spectrum, scSHC, CHOIR, RACEID, Seurat-SLC) and need to fuse them into a single consensus cluster label per cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spectral-clustering-on-similarity-matrix))
- **spectral-clustering-quality-evaluation**: Use when comparing spectral clustering outputs of MS/MS spectra (e.g., MGF-derived clusters with charge 2-3 precursors) against peptide assignments to quantify completeness, incorrect clustering ratio, and clustered spectra ratio. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-clustering-quality-evaluation))
- **spectral-clustering-tool-configuration**: Use when profiling the runtime breakdown of CPU-only spectral clustering tools on large MS/MS data sets (e.g. PXD000561 MGF files) to quantify the preprocessing bottleneck before benchmarking a faster method such as HyperSpec. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-clustering-tool-configuration))
- **spectral-clustering-with-silhouette-k-selection**: Use when a consensus/aggregate similarity matrix (e.g., an SNF matrix from CHAI-SNF or an Average Similarity matrix from CHAI-AvgSim) must be converted into final cluster assignments, and the true number of cell types is unknown. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spectral-clustering-with-silhouette-k-selection))
- **spectral-community-detection**: Use when the input is a multi-dimensional adjacency tensor (S subjects x T time points, e.g. a 16 x 8 x 500 numpy array) and the goal is to infer time-varying personalized network modules, especially when subjects share little or no apparent information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-community-detection))
- **spectral-conditional-molecule-generation**: Use when you have simulated or measured molecular spectra (IR, Raman, UV-Vis, or all jointly) and need de novo molecular structure elucidation evaluated by top-1/top-10 exact match rather than retrieval from a fixed library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-conditional-molecule-generation))
- **spectral-connectivity-filtering**: Use when you have picked peaks (coordinates and intensities) from an INADEQUATE NMR spectrum and need to cluster them into networks representing individual compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-connectivity-filtering))
- **spectral-consensus-spectrum-generation**: Use when after extracting and filtering top-TIC spectra for a given feature (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-consensus-spectrum-generation))
- **spectral-constraint-optimization**: Use when a batch of GC×GC–MS samples has already been peak-picked and annotated (consensus spectra available from GcDUO module III) and you need sample-wise peak areas/intensities that account for between-sample retention-time shifts, i.e. module V data integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-constraint-optimization))
- **spectral-continuum-normalization**: Use when an extracted SEDm transient spectrum (from HyperGal scene modeling or standard PSF extraction) must be prepared for SNID template typing, or when extraction quality is scored via continuum-divided spectral RMS over 4000–8000 Å. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/spectral-continuum-normalization))
- **spectral-continuum-subtraction**: Use when preprocessing 1D FITS spectra (e.g. from the AAOmega spectrograph) before cross-correlation redshifting, so that continuum-dominated flux is removed and only feature information enters the autoz cross-correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/spectral-continuum-subtraction))
